local _flight = shared._barrageLoaderFlight
-- Only an in-flight run blocks; the loader itself tells an open hub apart from a closed one.
if type(_flight) == "table" and _flight.Running and os.clock() - (_flight.StartedAt or 0) < 600 then
	pcall(function()
		game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "BARRAGE", Text = "BARRAGE is still loading. Give it a moment.", Duration = 5 })
	end)
	return
end
_flight = { Running = true, Ready = false, StartedAt = os.clock() }
shared._barrageLoaderFlight = _flight
